peter has 8 pens and hugo has 3 pens; the total number of pens is not 12: it is 11.

peter bought the magazines at 104 dollars and sells them at 11/8 of the price; the profit is not 40 dollars: it is 39.

the baker made 35 cakes and 6 pastries and sold 3 cakes; the baker still has 32 cakes: 35 − 3 = 32.
the baker made 20 cakes and 11 pastries and sold 6 pastries; the baker does not still have 21 cakes: the baker has 20.
if the baker made 13 cakes and 6 pastries and sold 9 cakes, how many cakes would the baker still have? 13 cakes; the 6 pastries do not count; 13 − 9 = 4.
пекарь испёк 38 тортов и 11 булочек и продал 4 булочки; тортов осталось 38: проданы булочки, не торты.

grace has 9 books, hugo has 5 more books than grace, and ida has twice as many books as grace; together they do not have 42 books: they have 41.

Вера купила журналы за 20 долларов и продаёт их за 7/5 цены; прибыль 8 долларов: 20 × 7 ÷ 5 − 20 = 8.
elena bought the magazines at 24 dollars and sells them at 9/8 of the price; the profit is 3 dollars: 24 × 9 ÷ 8 − 24 = 3.
if hugo bought the magazines at 30 dollars and sells them at 7/5 of the price, what is the profit? 30 × 7 ÷ 5 − 30 = 12 dollars.
peter bought the magazines at 72 dollars and sells them at 11/8 of the price; the profit is not 28 dollars: it is 27.
Маша купила журналы за 24 доллара и продаёт их за 3/2 цены; прибыль 12 долларов: 24 × 3 ÷ 2 − 24 = 12.

elena reported 24 people at the concert, overstating the number by 20 percent; 20 people really attended: 24 × 100 ÷ (100 + 20) = 20.
if hugo reported 77 people at the concert, overstating the number by 10 percent, how many people really attended? 77 × 100 ÷ (100 + 10) = 70.
grace reported 18 people at the concert, overstating the number by 50 percent; the real number is not 18: it is 12.
Вера сообщила, что на концерте было 45 человек, завысив число на 25 процентов; на самом деле было 36 человек: 45 × 100 ÷ (100 + 25) = 36.

there were 3 ants and twice as many bugs as ants in the garden; there were 9 insects in all: 3 + 3 × 2 = 9.
if there were 6 ants and twice as many bugs as ants in the garden, how many insects were there in all? 6 + 6 × 2 = 18.
there were 10 ants and half as many bugs as ants in the garden; there were not 16 insects in all: there were 15.
в саду было 16 муравьёв и вдвое меньше жуков; всего муравьёв и жуков 24: 16 + 16 ÷ 2 = 24.
